# Function Map — D2Game / D2Common 1.13c → TS server (draft)

Status legend: **diff-verified** = covered by a real-DLL differential suite or oracle golden scenario with
no divergence in the relevant columns; **ported** = implemented in `src/d2kern-113c/`, awaiting direct
real-DLL differential trace/snapshot execution; **diff-observed** = exercised by a golden scenario,
divergence open (see `P4-first-divergence.md` / `parity-baseline.json`); **unverified** = mapped by reading
code only.

Evidence for addresses: `kb/engine/oracle-hooks-113c.md` and the kb documents listed per row.
This table is meant to grow one row per original function as P4 iterations proceed; the long-term
metric is the share of rows in **diff-verified**.

> **Manifest Status Accounting (`manifest-113c.json` / `src/d2kern-113c/`)**: `manifest-113c.json` binds
> the **`4,810` reachable `D2Game.dll` (`3,331`) + `D2Common.dll` (`1,479`)** room-tick/packet closure
> functions (`4,626` Tarjan SCCs across `31` topological layers `0..30`, `45` binary-verified dispatch
> tables, `49` pruned host/CRT/Realm targets, `721` `D2Game` + `880` `D2Common` outside closure) strictly
> 1-to-1 against `src/d2kern-113c/**/*.ts`: **`3,128` `diff-verified` (`65.03%`) + `1,682` `ported` (`34.97%`) =
> `4,810` implemented closure functions** (`0` `unported`, **`0` unblocked `ready`** = `0`
> `readyPureLeaf` + `0` `readyHostImport` + `0` `readyInternalComposite`). Startup `.bin` loaders
> (`0x6FDC3330`, `0x6FDB0A80`), outer loop / standalone persistent-event wrappers (`0x6FC4E460`,
> `0x6FCAE940`, `0x6FCAE960`), and the external 11-slot `D2Net.dll` IAT boundary
> (`0x6FD18B48..0x6FD18B70`) sit outside the room-tick closure. Earlier rows mapped only to legacy
> `src/server/`, `src/client/`, or `src/common/` modules (`52` closure functions) remain
> `status: "unported"` in `manifest-113c.json` with `legacyRef` metadata until ported into
> `src/d2kern-113c/`.

Golden scenarios: 13 (`a1-town-walk`, `a1-bloodmoor-melee` and the 11 `p52-*` combat scenarios; ratchet in
`tests/fixtures/oracle/golden/parity-baseline.json`). Rows that say "all 10 scenarios" are from P4 and
still hold for the 13 scenarios.
